=== instructions carrying immediate 256 in candidate functions ===

=== interpretation guard ===
A write of 0x01000100, or two 0x100 channel fields, specifically to B2Y +0x0C/+0x10 would prove 256 as reset/default unity. Generic 0x100 constants elsewhere do not.

=== target ===
Identify direct reset/init values for gain words 0x2002008C and 0x20020090. If absent, mark hardware-unity reset as not recovered and proceed to datapath/FPGA evidence.
